[[Şırnak]] [[Lug]] [[Bingöl]] [[Mwah.]] [[Adana]] [[Pain]] 7) Ayla Akat Ata [[Batman]] [[Bitlis]] [[Diyarbakır]] [[Diyarbakır]] [[Diyarbakır]] [[Hakkari]] [[Hakkari]] 14) The Leader of the Secret [[İstanbul]] [[İstanbul]] [[Kars]] [[Mardin]] [[Mersin]] [[Mwah.]] [[Şanlıurfa]] [[Van]] [[Van]] Duststone is of great importance to the world ecosystem. The transport of dust particles from the deserts (such as Sahara, Arabia) and semi-dry areas is important for human health with climate, land and sea ecosystems. The predictions with the dust-moving models developed for this purpose yield positive results in determining the pathways of dust transport and taking necessary measures. The World Meteorology Agency has created the Sand and Dust Storm Advice and Evaluation System within the Atmospheric Research and Environment Program. This system aims to develop the capacity of member states to make realistic forecasts of sand and dust storms. In our country, dust forecasts produced by the General Directorate of Meteorology are published in the Internet. The main cause of dust removal is drought and desertification. The largest and most important dust sources in the world are Africa (Saharan Desert), Asia (Gobi and Takakan), the Arabian Peninsula, the deserts in South America and Australia. Dust from the deserts travel long distances, rising to the upper layers of the atmosphere. The relatively large dust particles collapse near the source areas, while the smaller ones travel thousands of miles. Dust and sand storms from drought and desertification have a negative effect on the daily lives of exposed people. People with respiratory and heart problems and older and children are best known to be affected by these events. Studies in Africa have shown that there is a key link between the cases of meningitis in children and dust storms. Dust storms also have a negative effect on air and sea transport. In areas close to dust source areas, airports are shut down during storms, and land-road transport is in great trouble. Turkey is affected by desert dust from the Middle East and Africa. The dust stone to our country is more intense in spring, summer and autumn, when the Basra Lower Pressure Center is more effective. Our Southeast Anatolian region, particularly our settlements near the border with Syria and Iraq, has been significantly affected by the removal of dust. In addition, the Intergovernmental Climate Change Panel (IPCC) accepts dust as a very important component of the atmospheric (aerosol), one of the main climate variables. The countries in our region are severely exposed and severely affected by the dust storm. According to current climate forecasts, dust and sandstorms are expected to live more intensively as the drought event increases frequency and intensity. For these reasons, it is useful to establish a research commission to investigate and take precautions on the negative effects of the region's population during the dust transport in the areas of the dust.
